<summary>**平滑插值改进 消除其中偶尔的卡顿**</summary>
	<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#2d3253de2eb44a3d9d2fe8f5978e6854" alt="bookmark"/>
	首先插值移动的对象是其它玩家
	update时从A中拿出最前面的pos B,并对当前位置和pos进行插值移动,如果距离足够小则把B移除,换下一个pos
	把每一次服务器下发的position存储到一个 list A中
	这样能保证对象从服务器发来的一个个点之间进行平滑移动,而不会由于服务器点的不断变化而移动骤变
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/7368f8ca-b3b2-4405-a2bb-c41af8a0bc97/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=bed9b4f611c794cf6e3dabc64646b375b204b8441338e1d62ceb98b810fda8c8&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>**玩家位置同步方案**</summary>
	**客户端是对服务端的拙劣模仿**
	<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#b4011d3d5dbf41b2aa890085d3c22789" alt="bookmark"/>
	**同步操作而不是位置会让体验更好 **
	**操作+位置+绝对时间戳，服务器收到操作后对比当前服务器上的绝对时间戳 计算出玩家理应在哪  作为服务器后续计算玩家碰撞、射击是否命中的依据。**
	当同步移动操作时，客户端会将玩家的操作（如移动方向、跳跃等）发送给服务器，服务器再将这些操作广播给其他客户端。这样，每个客户端都可以根据收到的操作信息来更新人物的位置。这种方式可以有效地降低网络延迟对游戏体验的影响，因为客户端可以在等待服务器响应的同时进行预测和插值处理，使得人物的移动更加平滑。
	而如果同步人物位置，客户端需要等待服务器发送最新的位置信息，这样在网络延迟较高的情况下，可能导致人物位置的更新不够及时，从而影响游戏的流畅性。同时，同步位置信息可能会增加网络传输的负担，因为位置信息通常需要更多的数据量来表示
	同步操作引起的位置偏差可以通过位置包进行平滑插值矫正.
	**在例如回合制游戏或者棋类游戏，同步位置信息可能更适合。总之，在选择同步方式时，需要根据游戏类型和网络环境来权衡。**
	弱网丢包下的处理: 可以搞个主端操作队列 带时间戳的 上传给服务器，服务器收到后计算并把操作队列+算出来的位置发给别的玩家。 因为一般是udp发送，可能有丢包。 所以当服务器收到后广播给所有玩家当前队列，如果主端玩家收到后发现和目前本地的不一样说明丢包了，就从丢包那里重发一次。 如果发现服务器下发的和主端的一致则清空本地操作队列
	**同时操作队列可用于回放:**
	服务器保存玩家全部的操作队列 用于回放 查bug、供玩家查看回放录像等
	**性能优化:**
	距离裁剪:发现收到太远,不做后续的位置解析等处理了
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/5cf3f36d-1a9c-4099-8b59-488ac8a599ac/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=d642b070c17397687b924867f779c9a3b8c99557d82919cc3af3bd28ab737124&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>移动同步 客户端发队列 ds校验成功则下发 失败则拉回</summary>
	总的来说就是：<br>主端移动时上报一个记录移动位置的队列abcd给ds，ds做出校验，校验通过一个a就下发一个回主端， 主端收到后就移除a，然后继续填充e到队列里。依次类推<br>如果校验回包丢了没关系，当主端收到下一个校验成功的消息时，说明前面的都校验成功了<br>如果校验失败，则把客户端强制拉回ds端的并清空队列
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/6f084082-fa05-49b5-8838-f279a831014f/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=200b22a2d66ada71eeeb12a7a689fc794bf2f225ee12fde1cf1937356b201cfc&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<summary>**物理引擎的位置同步方案**</summary>
	和上面玩家位置同步的一起看 很多类似的点
	<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#30f9f9e01712499ba50eef29066166a7" alt="bookmark"/>
	前后台物理引擎不同步 必然导致球踢不到问题 
	想完全同步 必须用帧同步那种定点数计算+固定随机种子 但这样计算开销太大
	UE4用的physx引擎是浮点数运算，大多数物理引擎都是浮点数的，所以就可能碰撞结果出现不一致
	**内插值  **仅同步物体的**位置**和**朝向   **两个数据包间通过**线性插值（Lerp）**插入过渡数据。
	**外插值 **额外同步**线速度**、**角速度**、**加速度**和**网络延迟**等数据，在收到数据包时直接推测出下一个数据包的状态，从而降低延迟。 但预测在玩家突然转弯时肯定会出错。
	**导航推测算法** 利用物体位置及速度预测后续位置 如果和下一个包偏差了就插值回去。 分别插值速度和位置使表现更友好
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/27392baa-451d-4258-b482-5aed0536cb45/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=5760a383763dcb14a015de277232c8f629dd060e4e94d37fdca31959c99cde59&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<summary>**宠物跟随同步方案**</summary>
	宠物跟随  一般来说对位置精度没那么高  需要记录主人的前n个路点
	从宠物发射线到主人，如果没有障碍物，则直接走过去
	有障碍， 则沿着主人之前走过的路点， 一点点插值过去
</details>
<details>

<details>
<summary>**五子棋 前台实现思路**</summary>
	1.服务器每次都会下发整个游戏的操作序列 (一维数组), 内含棋子位置,下棋者信息, 下棋/悔棋/认输<br>2.客户端本地维护一个列表, 每次和服务器下发的一维数组进行比对(for循环), 如果遍历时每一步都相同,就只处理最后一步操作    如果发现最后一步前有不一样则清空本地的,  全部以服务器的为准<br>3.数据驱动表现
</details>
<details>
<summary>**安卓增量更新apk的方式**</summary>
	原理: apk 通常包含代码+资源两部分   但内核是代码, 资源可以抽出来
	整包下载apk: apk包含资源+代码两部分.
	资源可以放在  cache 和 files 两个目录
	cache下的会被清理软件清理掉. files下的不会
	apk卸载时,会删除这两个目录
	apk覆盖安装时, 会删除cache,保留files
	2.
	因此增量更新apk的方法: 抽离出apk内的资源进行 apk壳全量更新+资源增量更新
	把apk内的资源抽出来放到files目录下. 此时apk只包含代码.可以认为是一个apk壳
	对apk壳部分进行全量更新, 也就是全量更新代码
	对files目录下的资源进行增量更新. 也就是增量更新资源
	使用过上述方式进行更新后的apk包: 放在files目录下的资源无法再放回apk内. 后续大版本的apk更新其实都可以用 apk更新apk, 资源 更新 资源的方式
	未使用过的,也就是完整的资源+代码的整包: 需要根据2来操作, 也就是会进行一步抽离复制到files目录的操作
</details>

<details>
<summary>**多线程的应用**</summary>
	1.网络监听<br>2.日志文件定时写入<br>3.第三方sdk里的一些逻辑 例如安全  他们会不停从主线程读数据<br>4.检测卡死   开一个线程去检测主线程的framecount是否在一定时间内一直有在++
</details>
<details>
<summary>**写游戏日志**</summary>
	**日志加密：**
		利用异或运算连续两次可以复原的特性
		存储一个key， 所有byte和这个key进行异或运算。
		解密：再和这个key进行一次异或
	**性能内存优化:**
		1.异步写入：日志打印是开了个子线程去写入的
		2. 降低写入频率:
		移动平台下没有实时写日志，而是暂存在内存中。  写日志的时机：游戏暂停/切后台或正常结束时。 以此来减少写入频率
		crash下：利用了bugly的crash上报功能，额外写入了50行
		3.按优先级写入，根据日志紧急程度，可以设置不同的写入策略。例如，对于关键事件和错误信息，可以实时写入；而对于普通事件和调试信息，则可以采用延迟写入或者缓存策略
		4. 每次上报/打开游戏时都会新建日志文件。 上报日志时，会把几次日志合在一起上报 日志文件超过一定大小时  建立新的文本去写入
		5.远程日志开关： 把某个openid加入服务器白名单后， 对应openid的玩家登录游戏时会自动静默上报日志
		6.压缩日志
		<empty-block/>
		<empty-block/>
	<empty-block/>
</details>

<details>
<summary>**网络游戏中的对时**</summary>
	**背景:**
	多人网络游戏需要各个客户端在同一游戏时刻尽量保证游戏世界一致，即各个玩家看到的内容是一样的，才能作出正确的操作反馈。
	**对时方案:**
	众所周知，网络通讯是需要时间的，客户端要怎么获知此时此刻服务器的时间呢？有一个简单办法。
	过程如下：
	1）客户端发送的对时消息包含客户端时间c1；
	2）服务器收到消息后，记录当前服务器时间s1，返回对时消息，其中包含两个信息：c1和s1；
	3）客户端在c2时刻收到回包。
	注意，以上c1、c2是客户端系统时间，s1是服务器系统时间
	如果客户端和服务器都能够及时地处理消息，那么可以粗略地认为，对时消息在网络的来回时间rtt=c2-c1，如果再粗略的认为网络来回传输时间差不多，那么对时消息从服务器传输到客户端的时间为rtt / 2。
	假设c2时刻服务器时间为s2，那么得到：
	s2 = s1 + rtt ÷ 2 = s1 + (c2 - c1) ÷ 2
	由此就得到了客户端时间和服务器时间的映射关系。
	**提高对时精度:**
	上面的简单方法存在一些假设：
	1）假设了网络畅通、并且来回消息延迟对称。
	由于网络传播介质多样、来回链路不对称性、网络抖动的存在等多种因素，延迟通常并不完全是对称的。为了缓解这个问题，一方面要尽量使用udp协议来发送对时消息，因为tcp有拥塞控制、丢包重传机制，会放大传输延迟。另一方面，可以通过统计历史的延迟数据来过滤掉延迟明显异常的对时消息，一般情况下来回延迟越大，误差越大。
	还有一个简单的办法，通过之前的计算我们已经知道，客户端计算得到的服务器时间与服务器真实时间误差最大不会超过rtt。如果一次对时得到的rtt足够小，满足业务精度需要，也可以直接使用这次对时的结果。
	2）假设了服务器和客户端都能及时处理网络消息。
	计算机打时间戳的位置在应用层，受协议栈缓存、任务调度等影响，不能在网络消息到来时马上打时间戳，也不能等到网络消息真正发送出去时打时间戳。这两者都会增加计算误差。**对游戏开发而言，要尽量给网络和参与对时计算的线程足够高的优先级**。客户端主线程任务繁重，能稳定帧率就不错了，如果把对时的计算放到主线程，rtt将会受渲染帧率影响、增大对时误差。
	**所以为了保证对时的公平公正,采用子线程对时**
	**参考**
	[https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Network_Time_Protocol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Network_Time_Protocol)
	[https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Precision_Time_Protocol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Precision_Time_Protocol)
	[https://segmentfault.com/a/1190000005337116](https://segmentfault.com/a/1190000005337116)
	[https://blog.codingnow.com/2006/04/sync.html](https://blog.codingnow.com/2006/04/sync.html)
	[https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/ExecutionOrder.html](https://docs.unity3d.com/Manual/ExecutionOrder.html)
</details>
<details>
<summary>**帧同步和状态同步**</summary>
	<details>
	<summary>**帧同步**</summary>
		- **本质就是回合制游戏**
			你不做出操作（比如因为弱网）， 服务器就帮你做默认操作了（或者是由ai帮你做操作）
			可以理解为一个同步帧就是一次呼吸：<br>吸气：收集所有房间内玩家的操作请求  然后做安全校验后屏住<br>呼气：收集完成后 到达规定时间，就把所有消息一股脑广播给所有玩家，由各个客户端自己计算
			<empty-block/>
		- **帧同步一般选16帧或者15帧  王者是15帧**
			帧同步，最终要的是渲染帧和物理帧分离！ 物理帧负责接发协议， 渲染帧是实时描绘
			一般选取16帧，这样同步一次， 因为 16可以被1除尽，不会累计误差。 但是因为硬件渲染一般是30帧或者60帧，所以渲染时会累计误差（所以也有些选了15帧，但是计算逻辑时就会出现误差了1/15除不尽）
			<empty-block/>
		- **注意其中同步帧 逻辑帧和渲染帧区别**
			同步帧是服务器广播给所有客户端的  这个频率一定是固定的<br>逻辑帧指的是客户端计算的频率，一般都是收到服务器操作广播后开始模拟  同样是固定的<br>渲染帧是实时的，和上述两者无关  毕竟是表现层的东西.
			<empty-block/>
		- **无buffer的帧同步:** 
			平时我们看视频都会有buffer缓存,才能让我们对抗网络不稳定时的视频卡顿. 但是在游戏开发中这样的缓冲会带来延迟,导致有些客户端计算的快,有些计算的慢 破坏了帧同步<br>所以我们要确保每一次拿到的都是最新的信息,而不要在缓存中.<br>在王者荣耀里 解决上述问题  使用的是冗余的UDP(会把前5帧的包全部发过来), 用来确保客户端能拿到,并且如果真的没拿到就把之前那一帧传回来<br>服务器 15帧计算一次, 每次都把所有收到的客户端输入中取出最大队列(比如40个用户输入) 发送出去,剩下的等下一个15帧再取最大队列发送(就像一辆辆班车一样发送用户操作)
			<empty-block/>
		- **帧同步防作弊方法:**
			1.操作在转发前先做一下合法性校验<br>2.投票机制: 每一帧玩家的位置 ,血量,等级等关键数据在逻辑层应该是一样的(表现层当然它们可能不一样) 每一帧对它们做一次哈希并上传服务器, 如果某个客户端不一样就说明作弊了. 
			当然如果是因为代码问题导致所有客户端计算出来的结果不一样也会弹出作弊提示,这就考验代码功力了
			<empty-block/>
		- **网络波动**
			收到客户端广播的时间可能不同客户端也是不一样的 所以操作必须带时间戳 保证一致性
			<empty-block/>
		- **帧同步断线重连**<br>1.服务器下发所有指令, 客户端演算,此时显示加载进度.  等演算完毕后 玩家进入游戏.  也就是说: 游戏越玩到后期,重连时间越长.  (LOL 王者荣耀都是这样)<br>2.优化法: 内存复盘 (游戏崩溃/掉线前 会把当前队列数据全部存到本地, 重连时全部先恢复, 然后再追帧到当前游戏进度
			<empty-block/>
		- **客户端还是服务器做计算？**
			主流是服务器收集后广播
			也有完全服务器收集后计算后结果同步给客户端的做法. 但这样客户端锁帧时间就比较不稳定了.如果上报的操作比较复杂，那服务器算的可能就久一点，导致客户端卡帧时间久一点，体验不一定好.  但优点是安全
			<empty-block/>
		- **帧同步一些优化方法**
			![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/2bf19365-7a81-407f-b0de-6536d0e8087f/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=7f38bd316e9fcfa0bbc678bfe2390dc6b2295212b56af5bb5733cfcdda4332a1&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>**射击游戏第一人称第三人称实现上的的区别**</summary>
	其实就是个相机 + 人头是否显示
	第三人称在身后，需要处理相机穿墙问题。ue里用springarm解决。 第一人称就在手上
	防穿模： 第一人称下枪的depth设置为最高， 第三人称下tick里检测防止枪头进入墙里
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/0e4f59b9-15db-4d8a-90bf-7b04d14252cd/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=ef50f7cccac37230e299fc49b8ef44e42aa6025ff49ff0560ac57fe4d5f5df43&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
</details>
<details>
<summary>**FPS游戏中射击实现相关**</summary>
	- **影响枪械命中的一些参数**
		 后坐力 准心周围发散
	<empty-block/>
	- **射速补偿**
		高帧率下玩家发射子弹的频率可能比低帧率要高（**检测硬件层输入频率高**），所以做了射速补偿。保证高低帧率下尽可能射速一致
		<empty-block/>
	- **命中校验的内容**
		服务器校验：
		1.击杀上报校验：
		位置校验： 上报的子弹位置和死者的距离、
		玩家校验： 请求命中者当前是否处于开枪状态，死者当前状态是否可被击杀
		枪械校验：枪的状态机是否处于可发射状态
		子弹校验：对子弹根据玩家上报的开枪位置和死者位置做一个弹道模拟
		<empty-block/>
	- **命中同步 一定要有回溯计算**
		客户端命中别人后把shootid 和一些射击data交给服务器 服务器校验成功后广播给所有玩家
		移动中，对于主端：如果ds和客户端相差2m以内，则信任客户端，不拉回ds位置
		对于模拟端：使用延后表现，来确保模拟端是在插值前往正确的ds位置，而不是模拟预测法那样预测之后在哪。 （因为人会拐弯啥的，如果预测的还是直线体验会差很多)
		延迟补偿+<span color="red">回溯计算</span>：由于模拟端一定是在追逐别人真实位置的影子，所以打影子后服务器如何判定其是否打中呢， 会有个回溯计算，来计算真实距离， 同时人物头上的碰撞盒也是长方形的
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/0d752da2-f596-428b-9421-c0959d6e4329/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=23a29cf04357d41d18909984345c84d190b3cd78ec155971435f8b1371dca9bc&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

	<empty-block/>
	- **子弹命中要分为上报包和同步包差异**
		主端上报包 一梭子子弹以后 会以一个子弹数组上报 其中的每颗子弹都会带上命中的精确时间戳
		服务器同步给所有客户端的同步包 则只需要用一个命中数组也就是bullethitrepinfo 不需要带精确时间给到模拟端 模拟端只需要大致表现这么个突突突的命中飙血效果就行
		<empty-block/>
</details>
<details>
<summary>**资源加载方案**</summary>
	- **定义**
	加载分为 本地加载(缓存 本地ab资源）和远程下载(web www unityweb cos cdn等）
	远程下载的需要管理它的并发， 也就是不能同时有很多个一起下载，并且可能有优先级。 因此需要有一个统一的调度队列
	- **加载流程**
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/f564a7a1-53e3-4450-bad8-ada69d9ce4a4/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=ae2f1172679b0e96b874e24cbfd59259dc77a29d670b65250deaebb6a26ade7f&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	- **简化下载的逻辑**
		下载某资源时,如果该资源已在队列, 则对该资源再加个下载完成回调 而不在放回队列. 如果在下载中但是想取消它的下载, 则依然下载该资源但移除它的下载完成回调
	- **下载后的解压**
		网上下载下来的图片可能是压缩过的, 所以需要解压缩. 解压缩的话需要知道对应二进制文件具体是个啥、用的什么压缩格式等, 项目中是通过命名去约束的, 从下载下来的文件名里去解析长宽 格式等等
	- **懒加载 **
		先加载最核心的部分 后续根据用户点击和需求再初始化别的部分
		这里和预加载是相对的. 可根据性能和内存做权衡用哪种
	- **异步加载**
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/02f1cbcc-b615-4a6a-97e6-dd05bb4ca20e/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=5e01dedf91badd1e5f0e3e6f47e14a3a01416d932bc11e4b4c47ea73866c0922&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	<empty-block/>
</details>
<details>
<summary>**多线程下载防止资源竞争的方式**</summary>
	子线程回归主线程的思路： 子线程处理只置物体的状态， 主线程的update里去判断物体状态是否满足条件 满足才回调
	举例：
	多线程下载下，由子线程负责下载， 子线程下载完成后把对应对象的下载状态置位succ
	主线程里的update每帧去判断该队列里当前下载对象是否为succ， 是的话就从下载队列移除并回调业务层下载成功
	好处：如果不用子线程回归主线程， 那子线程随时可能回调， 如果主线程此时在处理的对象a刚好在子线程回调里也会处理， 那就会发生资源竞争！需要加锁等繁琐实现。
	因此子线程回调，主线程检测才是避免各种多线程bug的王道解决方式
</details>

	<details>
	<summary>**四叉树 八叉树 2d和3d的2分法**</summary>
		局限性:
		本质都是分治思想,<br>然后分割的目的都是为了快速查找<br>在3d中,其实就是为了:加快碰撞检测速度(ppc也是这个作用), 查找临近物体
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/ef9aef82-51cf-4ad4-8a62-77ef903903e9/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=e59029a973d9415e654c529159ce169445cc357d4cf32fa625dccce700187c11&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
		八叉树,其实就是把3d空间按一定规则递归把等分到一定粒度,每个节点包含八个子节点的树
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/f90c98b8-cf6d-4b23-bbc7-6929314c058e/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=5c53d247d91ecda0abad79badb3d866ec761f86e25c1c6239870ccb440104716&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
		对于解决大小不一的点的问题会很吃力
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/730b35cf-1675-4309-8b8d-ad6c8f083fec/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=d2969c0606e2805026764713132092f5be18ae521116201849045f0bee312348&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
		如上图,在构建过程中,一旦发现有个很大的点, 横跨了之前构建的四叉树体系, 对它的处理就会很麻烦. 一般是放到对应各自象限的父节点下. 但后续扩展四叉树时依然会很难搞. 所以四叉树和八叉树尽量用来解决相同大小的点的划分问题
		四/八叉树的话很容易出现生成的二叉树不平衡的缺点，特别是目标点都击中在某个象限里的时候  下面的BSP/KD树就好了很多
	</details>
	<details>
	<summary>**BSP 空间二叉分割法**</summary>
		<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#09b5050406b24df296caaf69dbfe4c9c" alt="bookmark"/>
		<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#ab124151ffdd4e708ec387f0b587ade5" alt="bookmark"/>
		离线分割好保存后，运行时可以用于光线追踪、视野可见性判断等。 判断一束光、一个视线这样的直线是否能命中某些面片 从而决定是否显示阴影或者不可见。
		和八叉树的区别是，它可以选择任意角度、位置来分割平面作为分割依据 而不是定死的从中间二分。所以可以划分不规则物体，更加符合实际。
		一条空间切过去后认为规定它的正反面，以此不断切割直到最基本的三角形面片为止. 建立起了一棵二叉树
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/866071c2-e9ca-46d6-bcd9-2a6a80a87a2b/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=eaa3725d25018e6476f8b7b3ddf1d6be65d34d6f0d3738c8714d60ec9bd0d59b&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

	空间分割的依据有很多种，可以根据实际需要来定。 <span color="red">**怎么让最后生成的树平衡怎么来**</span>
	以下是一些常见的依据：
	1. 随机选择：从空间中随机选择一个几何对象（如三角形、线段等）作为划分平面。这种方法简单易实现，但可能导致不平衡的BSP树，从而影响查询效率。
	2. 轴对齐划分：选择一个轴对齐的平面（如x=0、y=0等）作为划分平面。这种方法易于计算，但可能导致空间划分不够精细。
	3. 最佳分割平面：遍历所有可能的划分平面，选择使得两个子空间中几何对象数量最接近平衡的平面作为划分平面。这种方法可以生成较平衡的BSP树，提高查询效率，但计算复杂度较高。
	4. 启发式方法：结合多种策略，通过启发式方法选择划分平面。例如，可以根据几何对象的包围盒、密度等信息，选择一个可能导致较平衡划分的平面。这种方法在计算复杂度和查询效率之间寻求平衡。
	5.
	<empty-block/>
</details>

<summary>**预处理的遮挡剔除(PVS)**</summary>
	<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#2c0c5e58373f45ff91ed316d1dfc953b" alt="bookmark"/>
	预处理 先空间分割，然后对每一块区域，模拟相机在不同位置、朝向下哪些物体会被遮挡（这个粒度根据项目需求来) 然后运行时直接在对应范围里把物体不渲染，隐藏(需要注意从无到有和从有到无的性能开销,特别是OnVisible里有逻辑的)

<summary>**赛道描述之里程线方案**</summary>
	竞速游戏本质: 赛车 + 赛道<br>里程线的目的:描述赛道 : 用于记圈  复位
	1.原来的方法<br>用路点来描述赛道:
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/b69c2481-f3d7-464d-afd2-cab449022755/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=0b8cf0f3eb60e51cc199149b725fc51fed80552ee20506997268d5adde5da529&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	优点:实现简单<br>问题: 本质问题:用点不能充分描述赛道 引起记圈异常   复位点粗糙  到达终点不精确 复位点应该精确的在当前区域的正中央， 防止边缘卡住等问题
	2.平面描述法 也就是里程线法:<br>用一条条线组成的平面来描述赛道
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/25ebea00-5c7c-4f53-a20d-c7161c765c3d/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=1d6c29ca606e1f492296b69a8f60a98d63e944bf820292cc1bc33fac58b8d5cc&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	优点: 描述更充分, 可以有效.  复位点可配置
	领先落后判断：
	对每条线标明index,谁的index在前面谁就领先。 每次冲过一条线都上报服务器。 关键线必须冲过。
	记圈：
	如果从起点倒开，则圈数-1， 如果正开则圈数+1. 所有经过的线都会记录到本地日志中，用于查问题
	3.平面描述法难点:
	弯道:
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/89198ab6-ba97-43e2-8f74-4a5cccb639a7/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=007765b8a76369e765cca8b115fb55b45c9e5eca5abad2693552c73f47cc84ff&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	4.铺设 方法: 离线人工铺设, 简单赛道 0.5d, 困难赛道1d<br>铺设方法:<br>创建一个路径点预设, 里面包含当前位置index, 权重 复位点然后使用editor编辑器拉升成一条线，其实就是用了碰撞检测检测两边边缘形成一条线
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/5b84315f-7d76-4d3b-a147-9e5287c34e15/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=49fe2113cc7da40d7e40bd28046d97617df9ac1dd3033c7d1a505db2770df6ab&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	5.离线配置检查:<br>邮件扫描,复位点配置,赛道描述是否精确
	4.反外挂:
	因为都是客户端上报, 所以反外挂是必须的
	反外挂做在服务器上:每次客户端上报路点时,会根据上报点进行判断
	1.有些点是关键点 (例如起点终点  关键汇入点等等), 必须经过的,如果没有经过说明异常 , 关键点由策划在赛道场景中选取和配置到表里.交给服务器
	2.触发反外挂后,会有警告提示.对玩家强制复位/封号等
	5.对AI的支持
	赛道宽度 方向等信息充分, 足够提供支持
</details>

<summary>**自动化测试工具**</summary>
	利用各种执行节点去模拟人力操作
	UE4为例: Root力设置地图人数 然后后面是各种执行节点 常用的就算gm和延时 就能一键跑对应玩法了 在做某些功能时很好用

<details>
<summary>**RCR算法 实现透视反作弊**</summary>
	基本理念：如果通不过RCR检测，就认为两者网络不相干，ds不下下发对象。 从根源上避免透视挂
	实现细节：
	就是铺设各种方形盒子，然后根据不同种类的盒子，去计算A是否可见到B。
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/8aab00bc-bbdd-4b07-9edc-c99d8634f15a/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=74a7fa2f8843dde3cea09271b9854b87f8ca36545e3fea73ee219931bc75253a&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	盒子分为以下几种:
	1.绿色盒子：例如大厅
	2.红色盒子： 打开的门 或者窗户 （这种视野可以透过去的)
	editor下全部铺满后，即可运用算法去计算两者是否视野可见
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/89daa6d0-3be8-4286-bd86-f6b08cde446b/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074028Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=818b171709e6d222884eba11ba88f155158ca14c3b3723c4304dfa255a932abd&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	当然，一般都是算法先生成一波盒子，然后策划去剔除不合理的地方。 而不是全部手动策划搞
</details>
<details>
<summary>**周围视野内有哪些东西的一种实现**</summary>
	可以写一个视野组件，挂在感兴趣的物体A B C上。 然后A B C如果因为距离玩家近了，被网络相关性创造出来。 就在它们的tick里定时给玩家本人打射线，命中玩家就说明玩家可以看到这个物体。
	这个comp的owner就是各种感兴趣的物体

		在单机玩法中， 触发某些机关后场景中发射一个道具攻击玩家， 可以认为是有一个虚拟的玩家， 当玩家走过对应触发器时对玩家释放技能
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/62a727ed-ba70-4efd-bcf6-1bcb763823b7/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=d3a7e820279ec7767645d3445859fbf3a34bfe2f4ba1c1c1f9a02de403b24f55&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
		道具/技能 和 使用后的buff应该抽离开来，<br>因为道具是一个实体，在场景中会存在一段时间，道具与道具间相互独立
		而buff是一个状态，buff间可能存在覆盖 替换等情况<br>所以道具和对应的buff解耦合可以让框架更灵活和合理
		实际使用和蓝图做功能有点像， 客户端搭好道具框架后。 新增一个道具时： 前台制作道具功能节点， 策划把对应节点配置到表格中即可
	</details>
</details>
<details>
<summary>**指针索引法(句柄)存在于各种功能实现的**</summary>
	指针法：以小代大 用一个小标签指代一个很大的东西 传值的过程中传这个小标签
	应用例子：
	1.lz4压缩算法  传入的其实就是这么个小指针  等真正解压时根据对应指针解压
	2.ab包也是由头文件和内容两部分组成  头文件相当于指针   到时根据头文件指定加载一个ab包中对应部分ab而不是整个解压
	3.虚拟内存
	4.虚拟纹理
</details>
<details>
<summary>**主界面弹窗系统 保证弹出顺序的实现方案**</summary>
	用一个数组规定主界面系统的执行请求顺序
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/3c9c19f4-45c0-4a34-abe1-2d9a31626c22/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=2f297ee5f0398331b751604a5184edfcc78d54a5b6175aea31d0f97f0e4b2e7c&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	到了对应系统就去调用base里的拉取网络信息协议
	如果拉取超时或者拉取成功关闭页面后  就下一个系统的数据拉取.
	几种需要处理下一个系统的可能:
	1.发送超时是放行 
	2.服务器回包后判断后决定放行与否 
	3.回包因为切后台等没收到(可以加个定时器,到时间没收到回包就放行)
	缺点是需要业务层对这套有比较熟悉的理解，不然容易卡弹窗。 但优点是很容易查到是哪个弹窗因为什么原因卡了。
</details>
<details>
<summary>**房间系统**</summary>
	其实也就是建房 房间成员加入、变动 开始游戏等一些通用协议
	客户端和大厅间的通信，等正式开始游戏了，才是到pvp服务器上
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/37a27a58-8547-4b09-ae39-96114423c3a4/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=a4949840121e5bd9108b46d6bf8006d980bbdaa12e11c2d8b3ee453b54e591bd&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	.需要重点关注的几个细节点:
	1.房间内的断线重连
	2.玩家被踢的表现
	3.玩家在房间内对可能处于任何别的场景中的玩家发起邀请
	4.从局内返回房间时的处理(包括提前回来的房主踢人后, 后续成员如何处理)
</details>
<details>
<summary>大平台往下落 下面的玩家如何防止被卡住  给平台下面一个锥形碰撞体</summary>
	这样玩家就会被挤走 挤到边缘
</details>
<details>
<summary>**数据驱动的datasystem 架构**</summary>
	核心就是数据管理器管理所有数据 数据发生变化后会派发事件 感兴趣的系统则根据监听选择性监听  这样彻底解耦数据
	1. 包含元素 : 各个system 各个datahandler 一个 DatasystemMgr
	2. 各个system发送 接收消息, system收到消息后 派发给 DatasystemMgr
	3. DatasystemMgr对数据处理,(包括资源加载), 然后把处理后的数据再派发到对应的datahandler上
	4. datahanler处理对应数据,然后再派发到各个系统上
	5. 系统可以在初始化时对感兴趣的datahandler进行监听.<br>一旦初始化完成了就主动去对应datahandler上拿数据,不存在就不回调. 存在就回调 (Addmodelbinding)<br>这样其实就是 system 和 data层一起, 然后ui层被单独拆出来, 实现了逻辑和显示的分离
</details>
<details>
<summary>**传输优化策略 改浮点为定点**</summary>
	要用float型时比如 0.55 改为使用int型 55 ,到了客户端进行 55/100 操作  
	属于是帧同步里必备策略
</details>
<details>
<summary>**排行榜 分页拉取及相关问题解决方案**</summary>
	排行榜数据是一次请求全量下发的，而不是到某一页才去请求某一页。 不然会有下面说的同一玩家多次出现问题
	1.排行榜分页拉取的原因是：
		1.排行榜数组巨大，一条协议的数组长度可能没法做这么大。
		2.如果客户端分几次请求分页时，可能在第一页的末尾和第二页的头部可能出现同一个玩家（比如玩家从第一页末尾的50名掉到了51名，那么第二页也会拉取到他）
	所以之前的做法是分几次拉取， 一条到了再请求下一页的， 直到榜单全部拉下来。
	2.分页拉取的弊端是针对实时排行榜来说的： 实时榜单中， 一个玩家的排名可能实时变化，那么如果客户端分几次请求分页时，可能在第一页的末尾和第二页的头部可能出现同一个玩家（比如玩家从第一页末尾的50名掉到了51名，那么第二页也会拉取到他）
	3.优化过的分页拉取： 一次请求， 服务器给n次回包， 客户端收到所有回包后认为请求完成。
</details>
<details>
<summary>**天刀 雨滴效果实现核心**</summary>
	其实就是一张贴图  tiling成很多格子 每个格子里1\~2个雨滴 然后移动速度 位置随机

<details>
<summary>**简易铺路寻路算法 基于路点实现**</summary>
	1.适用场景：快速迭代开发， 对寻路精细度要求不是那么高的情况。    2d场景和终点位置固定的3d场景
	2.原理： 前期：铺路点， 建立图， 建立邻接关系。
	烘焙阶段： 1.计算所有相邻点的距离，保存到dict里
	2.用dijistra去算（效率更高 ）所有点到其它点的最短路径   dijistra每一趟都能得到一个点到所有其它点的最短路径   这样把所有点遍历一遍即可
	其中用visited表示每趟访问过的点
	如果是计算B到A  则等价于A到B 直接return 剪枝
	3.根据路径保存到表格中
	运行时：根据首末位置点， 从图中找到最近点， 分别建立邻接线引导过去，这里涉及到直接连接的优化，后面说这个优化
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/050d1bae-c685-4614-aa18-945f0af742b7/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=57261d1c1cd5a9ddfe3eade0c2d1105f16ce178d2abcab6da4cd2e3a3afb84e3&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	3.编辑阶段：策划铺点， 一键计算。   运行时找到离始终点最近的路点，连接到始终点上， 然后开始寻路，
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/f3d4264f-e44c-449e-8230-d27def73ebbf/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=260dfb0b3acf36fef9303b76c3e7b80e17a348aaa26682a3236a97ab5acb1f73&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	4.遇到的问题 0.手动编辑太繁琐
	1.性能/内存：寻路点过多，计算量大，卡顿等。 解决方法：由线取代点，通过tiling来铺过去
	2. 寻路线分布不均匀引起，原因：路点不规律，有浮点数引起  解决方案：路点在配置时需要用int，不要用浮点数
	3.
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/afb09ab8-ae48-47fb-a6f2-1df1b92ae839/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=a255b575a2338c697acc3f22cb2ed0235a795ba21453000a4975116701c577ba&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	首末点连线不精确的情况，可能出现绕着走的情况。
	以及明明当前位置和下下个点可以直接连接却因为路径图导致和下个点连接后再和下下个点连接出现从而出现拐点的情况
	解决方案：
	0.更精细的铺点
	1. 锥形范围搜索法搜索更优点
	2.最终解决方案：  运行时的预先判断连接性
	运行时发现离自己P最近的是A点  下一个是B点   那么在P到A之前先做一次P到B的预判段  如果PB可以直接连接  则判断B的一个点和C是否可以和P直接连接  如果可以就如此往下  直到和P不可直接连接的点出现
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/5281f94a-d048-4b63-b521-11283c2f1de0/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=9114ffea6352619930ca45b9d1a8b5cfba4cf99a9a6b32989e6a8a78dd015f62&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	这一步预计算的开销 对单个点而言
	A到B 到C
	原本： 对B而言  开销是：Ab连线 以及找B的下一个点C
	优化的方法：对B而言  ，
	如果AC路径直连检测成功  那开销就是：找下一个点C   并且减少了AB连线的开销
	如果直连检测失败  ，B的下一个点是C这一步可以缓存 .  所以开销仅仅是计算AC直连
	所以优化的方法在检测无论成功与否，都多了一步计算距离直连的开销.   但如果检测成功  那创建中间点连线的开销就没了   所以整体性能和内存开销增长其实不大
	5.性能和内存开销
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/4426021d-d950-41f9-a7d8-a841b7ef0b6c/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=3abd670fd2b19cb7819a697a1aa2b834b3b27138d361d3bb75031eed32610208&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
</details>
<details>
<summary>**碰撞中出现穿透或者粘住不动的原因**</summary>
	<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#843df50e620342e1a4e38ae02b83c889" alt="bookmark"/>
	穿透的本质原因 帧率更新频率间隙的碰撞被忽视了 这种问题,被称为隧穿: 在速度很快的物体发生碰撞时会出现直接穿透的情况  或者每个物理帧进行预计算 提前打一次射线碰撞检测 命中距离过小就不让车继续更新位置
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/41a10da0-ff3b-4c9b-828f-2bfd1463ad8e/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=a7aeeaf69d65f75b8197e857d59d216c94b6aa503a5b6333fc525faee00c94fd&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	因为其实碰撞计算不是连续的, 而是每一帧去算一次.  所以如果帧率很低的情况下,我们看到的汽车运动,其实是汽车在以固定速度闪现.
	因此如果一个障碍物,刚好出现在两帧计算之间, 汽车就会直接闪现过这个障碍, 如上图. 导致穿透障碍物的情况出现.
	解决方法一般有两种:
	1. 增加帧率, 也就是汽车闪现的频率变高,但总时间不变
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/c965c0c8-957c-45df-827d-06cf191e7e90/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=aa0e4fd80e25823d79e0424c646b40a18aaa8cbeb6860ec3a519b83567a5c4c8&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	2.把墙弄厚一点
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/45bd83d4-4c5e-43b4-8a71-bd96ab6dfc01/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=f37bba5b053eef76f194e086bccd107a11814ecc13044ac978b5872b8c48bde1&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	3.把车速调低一点,这个其实最优的解决方法.  一般出现这种问题,都是因为速度太快引起的穿透
	第二种问题:粘住不动,是因为物理引擎认为发生了二次碰撞引起的. 也是速度太快导致直接卡在障碍物里,然后会弹速度太小,到了第二帧时引擎认为又发生了碰撞.
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/35ff10ce-12e6-4dc0-b723-d223eca0d217/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=36ca5f05cb9eba7c9e82f04bba3a526e264fd66e9bd49d45270bb5a9085c5dda&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	解决方法也同上: 提高更新频率,或者减小车速
</details>
<details>
<summary>**格斗游戏打击感的一些要点**</summary>
	前摇后摇<br>2.被击中后的卡肉 硬直<br>3.命中后的镜头特写 或者镜头微微晃动<br>4.击中/被格挡的特效<br>5.音效<br>6.场景被破坏<br>**凡是符合玩家预期 让自己觉得爽快的那些都是打击感的体现 需要游戏经验去强化**
</details>
<details>
<summary>**垃圾回收GC**</summary>
	<details>
	<summary>**标记清理法**</summary>
		**标记清理法不会出现循环引用 只要和根节点没有连接， 就处于“游离状态” ，依旧会被清理**
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/a5fa27e8-2918-49c0-9c2e-8363053f835b/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=c705d74cb965ecd5a374d11e061c149bc831b7d33fdf2ef57bd2a15d11625ed8&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	</details>
	<details>
	<summary>**多线程gc**</summary>
		游戏一般默认都是主线程做gc<br>但是像ue这种也支持多线程做gc。<br>类似标记-清除法的天然就支持多线程gc。  主线程标记就行， 清除可以在子线程里分帧慢慢做达到性能优化的目的
		优势就是性能
		劣势就是可能出现竞争、死锁、代码复杂、调试困难等问题
	</details>
	<details>
	<summary>**双色标记法**</summary>
		<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#690f6b26ec0b4a82b76d27ec55219032" alt="bookmark"/>
		<unknown url="https://www.notion.so/63f466fbfa49488887ee58e5dd0f6f3c#dfc089f2d50140798144733e4f0d0348" alt="bookmark"/>
		lua5.1之前用的
		触发gc时，先全部标记为白色， 然后一个个向下查是否有引用， 有引用就标记为黑，直到全部遍历完。 然后标记为白色的可以马上被清除 或者开启多线程gc分帧清除（标记完了类似markpendingkilled 多线程或者分帧去清除都无所谓了)
	</details>
	<details>
	<summary>**三色标记法**</summary>
		本质就是多了个灰色， 说明本身被遍历但是还没遍历完子物体可达性的。  维护了一个灰色列表graylist, 下一帧继续从灰色列表里取数据继续标记。<br>其实比起双色来就是多了个中间态灰色列表， 用来缓存这一帧还没标记完想放到后面再标记的对象列表， 后续可以采用分帧或者各种技巧去优化这份灰色列表里的标记， 避免了双色的非黑即白引起的强制回收
	</details>
</details>
<details>
<summary>**CG中的SubSystem: Feature组件化系统 **</summary>
	其实就是**挂在GameMode上共享其生命周期的Component**
	和UE4自带的SubSystem很像, 类似GameModeSubSystem。
	本质是通过组合\>继承的思路，把本来全写在Gamemode里的东西以组件形式拆出来作为组件挂上去。

<details>
<summary>**游戏灰度的几种形式 **</summary>
	**按ip 按玩家id 按设备id 按比例等等**
	如果是在登录前的灰度， 例如资源更新 ，那最好就是按ip 或者设备id， 
	先内部人员灰度， 再核心玩家 最后开放 这样逐级
</details>
<details>
<summary>**位运算在玩家buff状态上的妙用 **</summary>
	**因为可能存在多个状态叠加，所以人物状态用的都是位运算**
	核心 利用位运算  异或做区分得到序列A  循环 1+左移找出A中为1的部分
	人物可能同时存在多个状态  如何快速找到其中有变化的那几个状态并加以公示呢？
	把人物状态看成一组开关 默认00000
	如果有变化就变成1
	那么假设状态从10101变成 11011
	做一下异或就能得到01110就能知道中间三个状态发生变化了
	再对01110进行for循环里把一个拿一个1去做按位与 为1则是有改变的那几个下标
	也就得到这几个下标处对应的状态发生了变化
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/b1fb93da-ffb2-4f1f-a2cd-eac3ff52cff6/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=28de0df433d44ac267cfe00f9c2e425ff02d43970efb2c2d2e9f9acf58079a82&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	调试时可以10进制转2进制看哪些是1。
	如下两图，图1变动前，图2变动后， 可以明显发现图2比图1多了一个状态50，第50位的状态为1， 那么变动的部分就是第50位
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/6b790d7f-1d75-47f2-9f6f-0fc1c38c2701/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=1e1951b08fa88c60ab84dbf80378749b1fb1c6157e3219c23d6bfe87fc17876a&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>**CG中的跨DS同步方案(大世界)**</summary>

	<details>
	<summary>**DS切线切换**</summary>
		旧DS上的数据在切线过程中把netguid+actor信息全部提交给gamesvr，然后gamesvr再交给新ds去spawn出来，然后在rep给客户端。 在客户端看来就是新老是一样的
		![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/a8f883c5-b2a3-4233-8539-632add33ec26/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=7acacd5afd5d4f51c3cbacfa46041c2bf2a48d346f6d0667ab2462e42b06dd3d&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
		切线处来回移动时， 不是立刻销毁 而是缓存。
		需要考虑负载均衡。
	</details>
	<details>
	<summary>**性能、流量优化**</summary>
		- 由于pick up拾取物可能多而杂，大世界里禁止生成，直接走奖励发放
		- doreponce手动同步复制变量 或者ActorReplicatro里自定义序列化改成仅首次复制、值发生改变时同步
		- doreponce 使用   一定是 (变量所在实际类, 变量名,实际实例)

	<details>
	<summary>**关键点**</summary>
		- **匹配成功后大厅机器本地拉起ds进程  ds首先加载一张空地图 用于建立TCP连接通信**
			<span color="red">很像实时解析器里服务器拉起解析器进程并利用trpc进行通信</span>
			加载空地图主要是因为ue的gameplay架构下都是基于场景的模式，所以ds进程拉起后需要一个空地图，然后在空地图的<span color="red">Gamemode初始化</span>里去进行后续逻辑，例如创建GameInstance、建立socket链接。
			![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/d931ebee-2e6f-4e25-a864-c664baa889d0/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=15ac22e41390dd93ae9d6ffa244f561c60aa9e022d642c85bf13640fc8cca7b9&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>单线程+多线程并行架构OpenMp</summary>
	本质就是动静分离 充分利用多核多线程特性解决耦合度不高但又性能开销大的
	游戏中性能开销最大头的视野计算 ai 移动计算等挪到一个多线程里去做
	每一帧分成单线程+多线程  单线程做大部分功能   完成后交给多线程完成性能大头的 多线程完成后回调主线程才能进入下一帧
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/694e9cf5-666b-4224-913a-8c835c659280/image.jpg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=82a4855a9b59d423a6074968aa9b05b5ae9b903794a36f80b29d5f955af126b5&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

	游戏运行时会把每一帧调用的方法和值写到一个int数组里写log文件中.  拿到log文件后结合对应版本的pdb文件解出对应int数据对应的函数是哪个 值是多少 也就是恢复可读性. 
	可以通过校验对应帧号的hash值确认是从哪一行 哪一个函数开始数据不一样的 
	当然hash可能有冲突但通过别的数据去冲突就行
</details>
<details>
<summary>翻越攀爬系统的物理检测</summary>
	攀爬系统 物理和动画是完全分开的  玩家点击攀爬后  会进行物理预计算 传入身高等参数  和前面的平台进行一次碰撞检测  并判断前面障碍物的宽度 凹凸等  成功后返回高度 攀爬点位置给上层  上层根据高度决定用哪种动画爬上去
	要拿一个红色的球做一次从上往下的扫掠 初筛哪些碰撞攀爬点可用
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/b39750a6-c1a3-4b33-8bd3-32eeadeeed73/IMG_3662.jpeg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=12528aa2d92fae459ec5112fdd8d40084e927409db899c25b75f07977dd2bb81&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>GM设计</summary>
	某个包具体能不能用gm 显示gmui ,应该由后台推送, 而不是靠dev/shipping包去做区分 不然shipping包都测不了功能了 跑不了后面才开放的活动了
	<empty-block/>
</details>
<details>
<summary>大世界场景管理和反馈系统</summary>
	就是在前期根据场景资源优先级划分好 然后提供调整lod  显示隐藏  加载卸载三个指令  在运行时  实时根据当前的fps去决定用哪个力度的命令调控fps使他稳定到目标值
	<empty-block/>
</details>

	主端移动时上报一个记录移动位置的队列abcd给ds，ds做出校验，校验通过一个a就下发一个回主端， 主端收到后就移除a，然后继续填充e到队列里。依次类推<br>如果校验回包丢了没关系，当主端收到下一个校验成功的消息时，说明前面的都校验成功了<br>如果校验失败，则把客户端强制拉回ds端的并清空队列

<summary>gmp局部绑定和全局绑定</summary>
