<details>
<summary>**DS执行某个事件后同步给客户端去执行的通用实现**</summary>
	也就是定义一个通用事件类，包装一系列参数。 每次ds执行后创建一个AInfo同步给客户端 客户端收到后通过OnRep去触发执行。
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/dab60217-1292-4ee1-a564-bf7a1d0364b7/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=c4c19fc829649a3dbef49adab2bee136a800960f45116b67d9629e699dc8a496&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	也就是对应上图的打包Action参数这一步
	DS执行事件后 发现需要让某些客户端触发执行相同的事件 通过UpdateActionRepData
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/02b13857-973e-4fec-9e68-40a215fffbee/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=04e762c52e2c1b79a17137a332ef5094027daa989d591b0244ba8adfd3fdc637&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)
	本质是在把一系列事件信息 + 本次事件唯一id 封装到ActionRepDatas里，塞到一个actor对象中（其实是ainfo 差不多)
	把actorinfo里的owner设置成对应的这些客户端后(这里参考:<mention-page url="https://www.notion.so/6e8e59d52e0d4cf1ae3d7aaca52afa9f"/>)
	<span color="red">spawn出来这些actor 对应客户端因为owner是自己</span>就会同步这个actor及上面的ActionRepDatas
	然后在OnRep_ActionRepDatas里根据事件id + 数据触发和ds相同的事件。 达到同步执行事件的目的
	![](https://prod-files-secure.s3.us-west-2.amazonaws.com/f117ffa6-9d35-4dfc-8a5a-12561e26967c/73417a01-b299-4988-ae22-fa4e0dcd11f2/Untitled.png?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&X-Amz-Content-Sha256=UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD&X-Amz-Credential=ASIAZI2LB4665WAXHO5O%2F20260422%2Fus-west-2%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&X-Amz-Date=20260422T074029Z&X-Amz-Expires=3600&X-Amz-Security-Token=IQoJb3JpZ2luX2VjEH4aCXVzLXdlc3QtMiJGMEQCIG0EcFXLXWgbleGeQozML9BNJLLEXWM1fo9Cnq7O21KrAiAVRbuBPFjF%2FJS%2BZMx8xbdrDM%2Fb9LvnBAwADviTVppleSr%2FAwhHEAAaDDYzNzQyMzE4MzgwNSIMLddFCB%2FCIsnmwCDgKtwDThe2dVyoRAyWoFJfSwTOsgef4dLP%2FuJ9vKviCXZwBT2QDPfj%2BbLTBt6igkCFQ2Lj6vmLxqtSa4XiUSa9m25pt0DX1NqAoFoYtucvCNvsrixYZ4vWAJmoUxTUu5VITYUj%2FH%2BHK9j0INtnWbmPWUgFVzsCMAsoszbMs%2F2sFZctcQrzs1L5NA1o7pPG1M554GS4c9ajwyyoFzwAl9lV9Lp8GyESbAb9o5IBsGrle0JkIib%2F5uTOuvEDp7F1IZbn3nMCPgoUvXqTcD5ol43Awibfr5CJ11mAAwMSh81lkhdApuWx%2FzHNjxVBXHyrfY2S%2FU8WJu8xYwSNDzQ88NL6MYBPUDnR%2Bz9nVM9PfVcA0eOVa4dfZJhTPep8W6DkfQBEofMFtLp2rny4z%2B9%2Bd6QtUzj56L1NUCyPO9Y2iKaNF05cszm9m9RxrCH5gOsjR82Dh1epN6w36YEKx5SZakUFdJBHsUXAyZEvLkNDkSmpUbog75sqrvVOyTObSJRzm8FAYxfOUZOZFPy8fA8ZuoqsMhKXcpuJTeipAxxkPiE4xukvVhvaFU8nT8YB959B4NLI6sDUWz40AkkkifcpeuB6F7JM3HD2JmWImoIwsb063TbIuoV8DkpYaV4ROkNk9xMwh7yhzwY6pgGPBehrusoi%2B%2B1FTWSZmstZTXRxIKxHWfadA9m%2BY1AoeQc10MtjSjPEY16xZr8nk7NI6lCOPBj9vr1hBQiyk3PT6ArtgnYgLXQRPA4sgIGKgNB9bklB1dI6GtlgxbOIMwxIWRJPF2POYezSuS1C1Zic%2BzcfhRiWRPupa1O1E7RCyEFfKF70WzBvWJDNgDG32SLLfm3vR5tUf0rwDYRMsBj%2BD18Gsna1&X-Amz-Signature=b31369018553c1205533b78eeeb505ede0a8955d9d4c89c1c41d735287f87230&X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&x-amz-checksum-mode=ENABLED&x-id=GetObject)

<details>
<summary>**UGC功能**</summary>
	其实就是轻型的ue编辑器, 开放lua 客户端和ds逻辑都是写lua . 如果遇到编辑器内部的问题, 就只能依仗我们来查了
</details>
<details>
<summary>cvar灰度开关表</summary>
	控制一些cvar开关只在特定模式生效  并且有灰度万分比  想在这些模式下全部放开 百分比就填1w

<details>
<summary>定时器 不准问题 tick改小点去判断</summary>
	定时器不准是因为卡顿引起, 例如客户端loading阶段 服务器就已经开始计时了
	解决方案是要么根据某些不卡时的时点去开定时器 要么就改小间隔定时器里面再去判断是否到统计间隔了
</details>

<details>
<summary>不规则区域判定: 利用高度图+rgb检测提前生成不规则区域数据, 在运行时读取数据+四叉树判定不规则区域是否命中 </summary>
	应用场景:
	赛事地图排水功能(排除水域去刷圈)
	荣都双毒圈的外面那一圈  内圈依然是规则的圆
	地铁空投掉落区域
	优点: 基于离线网格数据+四叉树快速精准选点 不用多次尝试. 且应用场景不止地图选点 荣都不规则毒圈  大战场区域 其实都能用这个判断.
	缺点: 每次需要美术出图→策划用工具生成提交数据才能用, 可能地图改了没及时同步或使用就会出错.
	[https://tapd.woa.com/CJGame/markdown_wikis/show/#1220386762002649777](https://tapd.woa.com/CJGame/markdown_wikis/show/#1220386762002649777)
	让美术出图(4096x4096) 蓝色都是障碍区域(水域或者其他危险区域)  绿色是可以行走区域

	然后利用工具 读取美术出的高度图. 并归一化颜色,因为美术出的图可能没那么精确.
	所以把近似像素的同意归成蓝色或者绿色. 可以等价于一个网格里要么是0(蓝色)要么是1(绿色)

	提交p4后 等运行时加载这份四叉树文件 根据玩家(毒圈选点)的x/y值进行精确的找到对应数据里的位置, 判断是否可通行.
	<empty-block/>
	<empty-block/>
	粒度问题 
	目前4096 x 4096的网格, 在荣都里用于边界判断, 大概一个网格对应2米. 可以符合需求. 在毒圈边界能大体判定成功. 人走一步路就差不多了
